    1                  %   NORD-10/CE MICROPROGRAM  -  4k x 32 bit ROM
    2                  %
    3                  %   Words 0000-1777 are the standard NORD-10 micro program of ND-06.010.01,
    4                  %   with the labels, comments and mnemonic order of that manual's listing
    5                  %   (see NORD-10.uc for how they were read). Words 2000-7777 are the
    6                  %   NORD-10/CE extension, for which no listing is known: they are the lossless
    7                  %   disassembly of the PROM dump REFERENCE/NORD-10-CE-4K-prom4k.hex, with
    8                  %   every jump target given the name L<address>. The four user instructions
    9                  %   USER0..USER3 (op codes 140100, 140300, 140500, 140700) enter at 2200-2203.
   10                  %
   11                  %   Every line assembles to exactly the word the dump holds
   12                  %   (tests/NDMicroAsm.Tests/TestND10RoundTrip.cs checks that). The one word where the two
   13                  %   dumps differ, 1064, reads IARM PLUS here, as the listing prints it.
   14                  %
   15  0000  80000302  0000  L0000,  JMP 1402                          %STOP
   16  0001  80000301  0001          JMP 1401                          %MASTER CLEAR
   17                  
   18                  0002  %ROUTINE TO CONVERT FROM FLOATING NUMBER IN T,A,D-REG,
   19                  0002  %TO INTEGER NUMBER IN A-REG
   20                  0002  %D-REG AND T-REG IS SET TO ZERO
   21  0002  03000569  0002  DNZC,   ARM PLUS A,DH B,T D,A             %T + ΔH -> A
   22  0003  03004FA5  0003          ARM PLUS D,SS B,B4 A,A            %A + 20 -> SS
   23  0004  3D00E0AF  0004          LOGM AND B,B16 A,SS               %TEST BIT 16 IN SS-REG
   24  0005  80008177  0005          CJMP ZERO ZTAD                    %JMP IF OVERFLOW
   25  0006  3D00E1A5  0006          LOGM AND D,D B,B16 A,A            %TEST BIT 16 IN A-REG
   26  0007  8000C0E3  0007          CJMP NZERO FETCZ                  %JMP IF OVERFLOW
   27  0010  35000B05  0010          LOGM ADIR A,A D,SC                %SET SHIFTCOUNT
   28  0011  C000C000  0011          LOOP SHR SHZE TSC0                %SHIFT RIGHT TO SC = 0
   29  0012  3F000590  0012          LOGM BDIR B,SH D,A                %SH -> A
   30  0013  3F000060  0013          LOGM BDIR B,T                     %TEST SIGN
   31  0014  1D00D505  0014          CARM SNEG BMINA A,A B,Z D,A       %INVERT IF SIGN NEG
   32  0015  35600600  0015          LOGM CFC ADIR A,Z D,T             %0 -> T, FETCH
   33  0016  01400270  0016  SRB,    ARM BM1 B,X D,P CPTR              %STORE BLOCK, X -1 -> CP
   34  0017  3500020E  0017          LOGM ADIR D,P A,SP                %UNSAVE CP
   35  0020  7503800E  0020          ILOGM ADIR ORIN2 A,SP             %READ SP SPES, LEV
   36  0021  2180000E  0021          LOGM CWR1 A,SP                    %STORE SP
   37  0022  75038007  0022          ILOGM ADIR ORIN2 A,X              %READ X
   38  0023  0180000E  0023          ARM CWR1 A,SP
   39  0024  75038006  0024          ILOGM ADIR ORIN2 A,T              %READ T
   40  0025  0180000E  0025          ARM CWR1 A,SP
   41  0026  75038005  0026          ILOGM ADIR ORIN2 A,A              %READ A
   42  0027  0180000E  0027          ARM CWR1 A,SP
   43  0030  75038001  0030          ILOGM ADIR A,D ORIN2              %READ D
   44  0031  0180000E  0031          ARM CWR1 A,SP
   45  0032  75038004  0032          ILOGM ADIR ORIN2 A,L              %READ L
   46  0033  0180000E  0033          ARM CWR1 A,SP
   47  0034  75038008  0034          ILOGM ADIR ORIN2 A,S              %READ STATUS
   48  0035  0180000E  0035          ARM CWR1 A,SP
   49  0036  75038003  0036          ILOGM ADIR ORIN2 A,B              %READ B
   50  0037  2180000E  0037          LOGM CWR1 A,SP
   51  0040  01600000  0040          ARM BM1 CFC                       %FETCH
   52  0041  3500020D  0041  MINC,   LOGM ADIR A,R D,P                 %R -> CP
   53  0042  01000220  0042          ARM BM1 B,P D,P                   %CP -1 -> CP
   54  0043  1B400C0B  0043          ARM CPTR PLUS ADD1 A,H B,Z D,SCR  %CP -> R  H + 1 -> SCR
   55  0044  3E80029C  0044          LOGL CWR1 BDIR A,SCR B,SH D,P     %SCR -> (R + 1), SH(L) -> CP
   56  0045  1B608202  0045          CARM ZERO CFC PLUS ADD1 A,P B,Z D,P %CP + 1 -> CP
   57  0046  2B070000  0046  SWPCC,  LOGM ADIRC ORSW3                  %ZERO:CP + 1 -> CP
   58  0047  35040E00  0047  SWPC,   LOGM ADIR ORSKP D,SP              %REG -> REG (ONE'COMPL)
   59  0050  07070000  0050          ARM ORSW3 BDI
   60  0051  3566F00E  0051          LOGM CFC ORSW2 COND CO17 ADIR A,SP

  659                  1146  %ROUTINE TO PRINT OCTAL NUMBERS
  660                  1146  %PRINTS ON OPCOMDEV
  661                  1146  %NUMBER IN SH-MOST SIGN.
  662  1146  5B10EC0B  1146  OUT8,   IARM PLUS ADD1 A,H LE16 D,SCR DSPL %SAVE RETUR

  687                  1176  %THIS ROUTINE READS AN OCTAL NUMBER
  688                  1176  %RETURN :OCTAL NUMBER IN SH(MOSTS)
  689                  1176  %TERMINATING CHARACTER IN A-REG
  690  1176  5B10EC0B  1176  ASS8,   IARM PLUS ADD1 A,H B,Z D,SCR LE16 DSPL %SAVE RETUR.

  723                  1236  %THIS ROUTINE TESTS PANEL BUTTONS  AND
  724  1236  3D00F0AF  1236  PANTT,  LOGM AND B,B17 A,SS               %TEST LOAD
  725  1237  8000C3CF  1237          CJMP NZERO RPANT                  %JMP TO INCH

  914                  1501  %ENTRY IN MOPC AFTER $, & OR LOAD COMMAND
  915                  1501  %(SH1)= ALD-NUMBER, SPECIFIED ON CONSOLE DEVICE
  916                  1501  %IF NO ALD NUMBER IS SPECIFIED, THE DEFAULT NUMBER IS READ FROM ALD
  917                  1501  %ALD NUMBER FORMAT
  918                  1501  %BIT 15 : EXTN, EXTENDED LOAD FUNCTION
  919                  1501  %BIT 14 : RESTART
  920                  1501  %BIT 13 : MASS STORAGE LOAD
  921                  1501  %BIT 12 : OTAL LOAD
  922  1501  3F00C5A0  1501  DOLL,   LOGM BDIR D,A B,B14               %10000 -> A, AFTER $
  923  1502  80000345  1502          JMP DOLET
  924  1503  3F00FFA0  1503  LOAD,   LOGM BDIR B,B17 D,SS              %SET LOAD-FLAG, RESET OCTAL READ FLAG
  925  1504  3F000500  1504  ETSGN,  LOGM BDIR D,A B,Z                 %0 -> A, AFTER & OR LOAD
  926  1505  3500000A  1505  DOLET,  LOGM ADIR B,PAS A,IO              %READ PANEL
  927  1506  010082A0  1506          ARM BM1 D,P B,B10                 %377 -> P
  928  1507  3D00022B  1507          LOGM AND D,P B,P A,H
  929  1510  370082A2  1510          LOGM OR D,P B,B10 A,P
  930  1511  35000A02  1511          LOGM ADIR D,IO B,PAC A,P          %SET LOAD BIT IN PANEL CONTROL
  931  1512  3D00C0AF  1512          LOGM AND B,B14 A,SS               %TEST IF OCTAL NUMBER READ
  932  1513  8000C34E  1513          CJMP NZERO DE0                    %JMP OCTAL READ
  933  1514  350000AA  1514          LOGM ADIR B,ALD A,IO              %GET ALD
  934  1515  3500090B  1515          LOGM ADIR D,SH A,H
  935  1516  37000595  1516  DE0,    LOGM OR D,A A,A B,SH              %OR IN B14 IF $ WRITTEN
  936  1517  3D00F0A5  1517          LOGM AND B,B17 A,A
  937  1520  8000C37C  1520          CJMP NZERO EXTN                   %EXTENDED LOAD FUNCTION
  938  1521  3D00E0A5  1521          LOGM AND B,B16 A,A                %RESTART FUNCTION?
  939  1522  8000C37E  1522          CJMP NZERO RSTRT                  %YES
  940  1523  3D00D0A5  1523          LOGM AND B,B15 A,A
  941  1524  8000C381  1524          CJMP NZERO MASS                   %MASS STORAGE LOAD
  942  1525  7510FF05  1525  DE1,    ILOGM ADIR D,SS A,A LE17 DSPL     %DEV. NR. -> SS LEV17
  943  1526  3F00FFA0  1526          LOGM BDIR D,SS B,B17              %SET LOAD FLAG
  944  1527  37008FAF  1527          LOGM OR D,SS B,B10 A,SS           %SET EXAMINE BIT IN SS
  945  1530  3D00C0A5  1530          LOGM AND B,B14 A,A
  946  1531  8000C23D  1531          CJMP NZERO NYFAF                  %OCTAL LOAD IF A = 010000
  947                  
  948                  1532  %BINARY LOAD
  949                  1532  %WILL PICK UP NECESSZRY PARAMETERS FROM A STANDARD (OCTAL)
  950                  1532  %BOOTSTRAP AND LOAD THE BINARY INFORMATION INTO CORE
  951                  1532  %IMMEDIATELY FOLLOWING THE CHECKSUM THERE IS AN ACTION
  952                  1532  %COMMAND. IF TERMINATING
  953                  1532  %CHARACTER IS A BLANK, THEN THE LOADED PROGRAM IS STARTED IN
  954                  1532  %THE START ADDRESS FOUND IN THE BOOTSTRAP.  OTHERWISE THE COMMAND
  955                  1532  %IS ACTED UPON BY MOPC
  956  1532  350000CA  1532  BINL,   LOGM ADIR B,MPC A,IO
  957  1533  800003EE  1533          CALL ACT                          %ACTIVATE DEVICE
  958  1534  35000900  1534          LOGM ADIR D,SH A,Z                %0 -> SH
  959  1535  3F000E90  1535  SEEK,   LOGM BDIR D,SP B,SH               %SH -> SP (POSSIBLE START ADDRESS)
  960  1536  350000CA  1536  SIKI,   LOGM ADIR B,MPC A,IO
  961  1537  8000027E  1537          CALL ASS8
  962  1540  3D00C0AF  1540          LOGM AND B,B14 A,SS               %OCTAL NUMBER READ?
  963  1541  8000835E  1541          CJMP ZERO SIKI                    %NO
  964  1542  1D0050A5  1542          ARM BMINA B,B5 A,A                %A - 40 -> AC
  965  1543  1B0000B0  1543          ARM PLUS ADD1 B,AC A,Z            %AC + 1 -> AC
  966  1544  8000C35D  1544          CJMP NZERO SEEK                   %SEEK FOR '!' AS TERMINATOR
  967  1545  350000CA  1545          LOGM ADIR B,MPC A,IO
  968  1546  800003A5  1546          CALL BIN
  969  1547  01400290  1547          ARM BM1 D,P B,SH CPTR             %SH - 1 -> CP, CP -> R, BLOCK START
  970  1550  350000CA  1550          LOGM ADIR B,MPC A,IO
  971  1551  800003A5  1551          CALL BIN
  972  1552  3F000690  1552          LOGM BDIR D,T B,SH                %SH -> T, WORD COUNT
  973  1553  35000100  1553          LOGM ADIR D,D A,Z                 %0 -> D
  974  1554  350000CA  1554  STLP,   LOGM ADIR B,MPC A,IO              %STORE LOOP
  975  1555  800003A5  1555          CALL BIN
  976  1556  3F000590  1556          LOGM BDIR D,A B,SH                %SH -> A, BINARY WORD
  977  1557  03000115  1557          ARM PLUS D,D B,D A,A              %ACCUMULATE CHECKSUM
  978  1560  01800665  1560          ARM BM1 D,T B,T CWR1 A,A          %T - 1 -> T, STORE A IN (R) + 1
  979  1561  8000C36C  1561          CJMP NZERO STLP
  980  1562  350000CA  1562          LOGM ADIR B,MPC A,IO
  981  1563  800003A5  1563          CALL BIN                          %READ CHECKSUM
  982  1564  33000091  1564          LOGM EXOR B,SH A,D
  983  1565  8000C25C  1565          CJMP NZERO QUM                    %CHECKSUM ERROR
  984  1566  3F00FFA0  1566          LOGM BDIR D,SS B,B17              %SET LOAD BIT
  985  1567  350000CA  1567          LOGM ADIR B,MPC A,IO
  986  1570  8000027E  1570          CALL ASS8                         %READ ACTION CODE
  987  1571  35000005  1571          LOGM ADIR A,A
  988  1572  800082C5  1572          CJMP ZERO STSP                    %START PROGRAM (TERMINATOR = BLANK)
  989  1573  80000241  1573          JMP REAC+2
  990                  
  991                  1574  %EXTN
  992                  1574  %EXTENDED LOAD FUNCTION
  993                  1574  %WHEN THE EXTN-BIT (BIT15) IN A IS SET, A MICRO-JUMP TO THE ADDRESS
  994                  1574  %FOUND IN A BIT0-10 IS PERFORMED
  995                  1574  %NO FURTHER DECODING OF ALD TAKES PLACE
  996  1574  35000AB5  1574  EXTN,   LOGM ADIR D,IO B,CAR A,A
  997  1575  A0000000  1575          JMP,CAR
  998                  
  999                  1576  %RSTRT
 1000                  1576  %RESTART FUNCTION (NOT TO BE CONFUSED WITH THE RESTART BUTTON)
 1001                  1576  %WHEN THE RSTRT-BIT (BIT 14) OF A IS SET, THE CPU IS STARTED
 1002                  1576  %IN 4* (THE ADDRESS FOUND IN BIT0-13 IN A ). BIT 0-1 IN THE
 1003                  1576  %RESULTING ADDRESS IS 0
 1004  1576  03000555  1576  RSTRT,  ARM PLUS D,A B,A A,A              %*2
 1005  1577  03000E55  1577          ARM PLUS D,SP B,A A,A             %*4
 1006  1600  800002C5  1600          JMP STSP                          %START PROGRAM
 1007                  
 1008                  1601  %MASS
 1009                  1601  %MASS STORAGE LOAD
 1010                  1601  %MASS WILL LOAD 1K WORDS FROM MASS STORAGE ADDRESS 0 INTO MEMORY
 1011                  1601  %FROM MEMORY ADDRESS0
 1012                  1601  %(LOWEST)  MASS STORAGE REGISTER ADDRESS IS FOUND IN BIT 0-10 IN ALD
 1013                  1601  %THE ACTUAL MASS STORAGE MUST CONFORM WITH THE DRUM/DISC
 1014                  1601  %PROGRAMMING SPECIFICATION.

 1074                  1657  %MAIN MEMORY CHECK
 1075                  1657  %MICRO-PROGRAM, VERSION C
 1076                  1657  %NJL 6/6/73
 1077                  1657  %
 1078                  1657  %TESTS MEMORY FROM ADDRESS SPECIFIED IN B-REG AND UP TO
 1079                  1657  %ADDRESS SPECIFIED IN X-REG
 1080                  1657  %
 1081                  1657  %ERROR INDICATION:
 1082                  1657  %    T=FAILING BITS          [OR6 ]
 1083                  1657  %    P=FAILING ADDRESS       [OR2 ]
 1084                  1657  %    D=ERROR PATTERN         [OR1 ]
 1085                  1657  %    L=TEST PATTERN          [OR4 ]
 1086                  1657  %    B=START ADDRESS         [OR3 ]
 1087                  1657  %    X=STOP ADDRESS          [OR7 ]
 1088                  1657  %
 1089                  1657  %IF NO ERROR IS FOUND T = 000000
 1090                  1657  %
 1091                  1657  %PATTERNS USED :
 1092                  1657  %
 1093                  1657  %    000001
 1094                  1657  %    000002
 1095                  1657  %    000004
 1096                  1657  %    ......
 1097                  1657  %    ......
 1098                  1657  %    100000
 1099                  1657  %
 1100                  1657  %    ADDRESS STORED IN ADDRESS (THIS PATTERN IS RUN 16 TIMES)
 1101                  1657  %
 1102                  1657  %    AND THEIR  COMPLEMENTS
 1103                  1657  %
 1104                  1657  %USE OF REGISTERS IN THIS PROGRAM :
 1105                  1657  %
 1106                  1657  %    A = 000000  USE T AS TEST DATA
 1107                  1657  %    A = 177777  USE ADDRESS AS TEST DATA
 1108                  1657  %
 1109                  1657  %    SCR = 000000  WRITE TEST DATA INTO CORE
 1110                  1657  %    SCR = 177777  READ TEST DATA FROM CORE
 1111                  1657  %
 1112                  1657  %    D = 000000 USE TEST DATA AS IS
 1113                  1657  %    D = 177777 USE COMPLEMENT OF TEST DATA
 1114                  1657  %
 1115                  1657  %    L =        TEST DATA
 1116                  1657  %
 1117  1657  35000500  1657  MMCC,   LOGM ADIR D,A A,Z                 %0 -> SS               INIT PHASE
 1118  1660  35000100  1660  MM00,   LOGM ADIR D,D A,Z                 %0 -> D                INIT POLARITY
 1119  1661  3F0006A0  1661  MM0,    LOGM BDIR D,T B,B0                %1 -> T                INIT DATA
 1120  1662  35000C00  1662  MM1,    LOGM ADIR D,SCR A,Z               %0 -> SCR              INIT STORE
 1121  1663  01400230  1663  MM2,    ARM BM1 D,P B,B CPTR              %(B) - 1 -> CP, CP -> R     INIT ADDRESS
 1122  1664  1B00040D  1664  MM3,    ARM PLUS ADD1 D,L B,Z A,R         %(R) + 1 -> L          MAKE DATA
 1123  1665  35000005  1665          LOGM ADIR A,A
 1124  1666  35008406  1666          CLOGM ADIR ZERO D,L A,T           %(T) -> L IF (A) = 0
 1125  1667  33000414  1667          LOGM EXOR D,L B,D A,L             %(L) -> L IF (D) = 177777
 1126  1670  3500000C  1670          LOGM ADIR A,SCR
 1127  1671  8000C3BC  1671          CJMP NZERO MM4                    %LOAD IF (SCR) = 0
 1128  1672  01800004  1672          ARM A,L CWR1                      %STORE L IN (R) + 1
 1129  1673  800003BF  1673          JMP MM41
 1130  1674  01C00000  1674  MM4,    ARM CRR1
 1131  1675  3300004B  1675          LOGM EXOR B,L A,H                 %((R) + 1) -> H
 1132  1676  8000C3CA  1676          CJMP NZERO ERR                    %ERROR DETECTED
 1133  1677  1D00007D  1677  MM41,   ARM BMINA B,X A,R                 %TEST FOR LAST LOCATION
 1134  1700  8000C3B4  1700          CJMP NZERO MM3                    %CONTINUE
 1135  1701  2B000C0C  1701          LOGM ADIRC D,SCR A,SCR            %SWITCH LOAD/STORE
 1136  1702  8000C3B3  1702          CJMP NZERO MM2
 1137  1703  03000666  1703          ARM PLUS D,T B,T A,T              %T + T -> T            SWITCH DATA
 1138  1704  8000C3B2  1704          CJMP NZERO MM1
 1139  1705  2B000101  1705  MM45,   LOGM ADIRC D,D A,D                %                      SWITCH POLARITY
 1140  1706  8000C3B1  1706          CJMP NZERO MM0                    %DO COMPLEMENT
 1141  1707  2B000505  1707  MM5,    LOGM ADIRC D,A A,A                %                      SWITCH PHASE
 1142  1710  8000C3B0  1710          CJMP NZERO MM00                   %CONTINUE WITH ADDRESS IN ADDRESS
 1143  1711  8000022C  1711          JMP MOPC                          %END OF TEST
 1144  1712  3500010B  1712  ERR,    LOGM ADIR D,D A,H                 %DATA READ
 1145  1713  35000E0D  1713          LOGM ADIR D,SP A,R                %FAILING ADDRESS R -> SP
 1146  1714  33000641  1714          LOGM EXOR D,T B,L A,D             %FAILING BITS -> T
 1147  1715  8000025C  1715          JMP QUM                           %MOPC ERROR ENTRY-POINT
 1148                  
 1149                  1716  %INCH
 1150                  1716  %INPUT CHARACTER (BYTE) FROM DEVICE FOUND IN (SS17)
 1151                  1716  %RETURNS CHARACTER IN A0
 1152                  1716  %*** SCR0 IS DESTROYED ***
 1153                  1716  %IF LOAD-BIT IS SET (BIT17 IN SS0) THEN THERE WILL BE NO ECHOING
 1154                  1716  %ON DEVICE-NUMBER (SS17) +4

 1203                  1767  %THIS PROGRAM STORES THE L-REGISTER IN THE LOCATION CONTAINED
 1204                  1767  %IN B-REGISTER.  IT THEN LOADS THE SAME ADDRESS INTO THE D-REGISTER
 1205                  1767  %AND FORMS EXCLUSIVE OR OF L- AND D-REGISTER IN THE T-REGISTERS
 1206                  1767  %THIS LOOP CAN ONLY BE TERMINATED BY PUSHING MASTER CLEAR.
